Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the tumors structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 542. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 94 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on tumors structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 542. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 94. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any tumors module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 48. The projective resolution has length at most 721 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the tumors decomposition. By minimality, the element a^48 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 721-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
